* DONE [#C] The consent gate is decorative against a live adapter: permission is granted per connection, not per question
:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       acat-08l
:LABELS:   []
:ISSUE_TYPE: task
:CREATED:  [2026-08-14 Fri 12:14]
:MODIFIED: [2026-08-14 Fri 12:55]
:CREATED_BY: johnw
:FINISHED: [2026-08-14 Fri 12:55]
:CLOSE_REASON: Fixed in 3b2ab57 and live-verified. Permission is now a function of the question under way (Exec.permissionByCode: .ack grants, every ask denies; Settings.permission overridable; Conn.asked/decisions record each decision with the question that provoked it; Config.permission now only covers requests arriving outside any question, set to .cancel by both harnesses). Plus the empirical counterpart: Artifact.fingerprint/WorkspaceDiff — the workspace is hashed after seeding and at the end, the difference is reported either way, and a run that performed no act (read off the replayed transcript, not the command line) and changed files exits 1 naming them. Negative controls in stub_adapter.py: --write-on-ask (the live defect's shape) must be denied and leave the workspace unchanged; --write-anyway must be caught by the fingerprint. LIVE: refusing run leaves the 323-byte seed byte-identical (md5 unchanged), with two DENIED Bash requests recorded — one of them the author trying to compile its patch in /tmp, OUTSIDE the workspace where the fingerprint is blind, caught only by the permission layer; consenting run grants exactly once during the ack and writes a 1090-byte parser, no strcpy, clean under -Wall -Wextra and ASan/UBSan. RESIDUE (filed separately): denial cost 0 extra turns in this run, contradicting the ~97k-token figure in the docstring, and the harness discards the usage object that would settle it; adapter substituted claude-fable-5 -> opus-4-8 with the narration concatenated onto a reviewer's APPROVE, which decodeVerdict then read as an objection and bought a revision round.
:SOURCE_REPO: agent-cat
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/src/agent-cat
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

Found by the live run on 2026-08-14 (workflow w5sz6gul7, live stage). SYMPTOM: in the REFUSING run (owner answered no, transcript has six turns and no 'tool apply' line, bill 6) the scratch parse.c was nevertheless replaced with the hardened 823-byte version (seed is 323 bytes, md5 f18314ef... -> 8e856c16...). Timestamps place the write inside the AUTHOR's draft turn (dir birth 12:03:34, file birth 12:04:08, author turn spans ~12:03:49-12:04:23), and it is a birth not a modification: write-and-rename, the signature of an editing tool. CAUSE: Agentic/Core/Acp.lean:271 'permission : Permission := .grant' is a single connection-wide policy that nothing sets; pickAllow answers allow_once to every session/request_permission regardless of the question's Code. To the permission layer, 'ask text model author' and 'act tool apply' are the same thing, so the program's ask/act distinction and the case ok { no -> done } consent branch are unenforceable against a live agent. Harden.no_ack_of_refused still holds — it is about acknowledgements, not bytes — which is exactly the point: there is no theorem and no runtime check saying 'a refused run leaves the workspace as it found it'. FIX (two parts): (1) make the permission decision a function of the QUESTION, not of the connection: grant only for the act (.ack), deny for every ask, and document the known consequence of denial (the turn completes with prose in place of an act, ~97k tokens, no protocol signal); thread the current question through Exec into Acp; log each decision in the transcript. (2) Add the empirical counterpart of the consent theorem: fingerprint the workspace before and after a run, report changes, and fail the run when no act was performed and the workspace changed. Cover both with stub tests. ALSO from the same run, separate issues: the adapter substituted claude-opus-4-8 (not in its advertised catalogue) after claude-fable-5 declined on policy grounds, reporting it only as prose inside the answer — so Conn.configValues is not a complete account of what may answer; and all three reviewers approved a draft that was a policy-refusal banner plus prose plus a fenced diff rather than 'a unified diff only'.

:CLOSE_REASON: DecidableEq evicted from csum_point (class axiom), idMat/pow/powSum/sumPow/retryTrunc, conv/convOne/MSemiring, pin, indicator/gate — all now classical/noncomputable, which is the design's stance. Payoff proved in-tree: MSemiring Prop (Trace ind) elaborates — the Mazurkiewicz trace monoid is now a legal panel key, with conv_assoc and total_conv exercised at that key. Supersedes acat-6pq
:SOURCE_REPO: agent-cat
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/src/agent-cat
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

HIGH (4 passes): [DecidableEq] on csum_point is provably vacuous at both carriers; it propagates through idMat/id_comp/pow/retryTrunc/Ctx/conv/pin/gate. Consequences: the meaning category lacks identities at function-typed objects; the Mazurkiewicz trace monoid (a Quot) cannot be a panel key. Fix: drop the binder from the class axiom, classical idMat, propagate removals. Supersedes acat-6pq.

* DONE [#B] DSL: no meaning function for the surface — state [[RawBlock]] and prove denote-after-check equations
:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       acat-22m
:LABELS:   []
:ISSUE_TYPE: task
:CREATED:  [2026-08-14 Fri 19:00]
:MODIFIED: [2026-08-18 Tue 13:05]
:CREATED_BY: johnw
:FINISHED: [2026-08-18 Tue 13:05]
:CLOSE_REASON: done
:SOURCE_REPO: agent-cat
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/src/agent-cat
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

abstraction pass, high; converges with deep-review's proof-scope observation. The DSL is the only stratum with no [[.]]: Syntax.lean:199/189/174 describe layout, not mathematical objects, and no theorem anywhere relates check's output to denote (nothing states `denote (check ...) = ...`). With no equation for what a construct means, surface shape was settled by rubric — which is how a case came to be spelled as two sibling clauses. Fix: write [[RawBlock]] : Bindings G -> Option (Env G -> Dlg Unit) (i.e. denote . check), prove one equation per construct using the Plan.Denotes machinery already in Denote.lean, and state the accepted fragment (an inductive predicate on Plan that check's image inhabits) plus the missing adequacy direction in Dsl.lean's 'where the proofs stop'. Constrains and is partially subsumed by the redesign [[acat-k28]].

** Comments

#+begin_src json
[
  {
    "id": 88,
    "issue_id": "acat-22m",
    "author": "johnw",
    "text": "Corrected signature and re-costed route, from the adversarially-verified tests-vs-theorems analysis (see acat-dsl-guard-theorems-dqz for the cheap extractions).\n\n**The signature as originally sketched does not typecheck against the round-16 checker.** `[[RawBlock]] : Bindings Γ → Option (Env Γ → Dlg Unit)` is missing three things the real `checkBlock` carries (Check.lean:514-515): the function table and the pending loop result are required arguments (both `Type 1`), and the monad is `Except CheckError`, not `Option`. The composite to prove is\n\n    ∀ (fns : Fns) (Γ : Ctx) (S : Bindings Γ) (pend : Option (Pend Γ))\n      (b : RawBlock) (p : Plan Γ Unit),\n      checkBlock fns Γ S pend b = .ok p →\n      ∀ γ : Env Γ, denote p γ = ⟦b⟧ fns S pend γ\n\nwith `⟦·⟧` supplied independently — and note there is **no `.revising` clause equation**: `.bind _ _ (.revising …) rest` emits nothing by itself; the loop's meaning appears at the consuming `.caseResult` (Check.lean:628-631, 669-683), so the meaning function must carry the pending result exactly as the checker does.\n\n**Effort re-costing, both directions.** Cheaper than feared: `denotes_revising` already exists (Denote.lean:493) with `reviseLoop` and the Acceptance theorems, and the three continuation obligations — `Plan.Denotes (checkCont chk) …`, likewise `reviseCont`, `finishCont` — each discharge in ~2 lines by `denote_sub` (each is `Plan.sub` of one plan, Check.lean:458-477). Dearer than hoped: the uncosted work is stating `⟦RawBlock⟧` itself, and the equations quantify away the checker's prompt/kind computation — either they take `Prompt.expr`/`bindKind` results as hypotheses (then they say nothing about the rendered words) or `⟦·⟧` re-implements those functions (then the agreement diffs two copies of one algorithm).\n\n**Consequence for the battery.** 28 of the 45 §9 discovery pins read a concrete rendered prompt through `promptAt`; those stay even after this issue closes. The honest end state is *thinning §9 to roughly one pin per construct as the fixture witness*, with the per-construct equations carrying the ∀-content — not deleting the section. The flagship's universal trace agreement (DslFlagship \"What is not proved\" §2) additionally needs the bespoke per-clause agreement with the hand-written `Harden.demo`, which no meaning function supplies.\n",
    "created_at": "2026-08-16T00:22:04Z"
  },
  {
    "id": 124,
    "issue_id": "acat-22m",
    "author": "johnw",
    "text": "Closed by the .wf retirement: the .wf surface is retired (owner ruling 2026-08-18, Haskell surface only); a meaning function for a surface that no longer exists has no subject",
    "created_at": "2026-08-18T20:05:37Z"
  }
]
#+end_src

* DONE [#C] DSL tooling: pre-run floor/ceiling cost report (Over/Under fold) with dry-run presentation and machine diagnostics
:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       acat-455
:LABELS:   []
:ISSUE_TYPE: task
:CREATED:  [2026-08-14 Fri 20:22]
:MODIFIED: [2026-08-18 Tue 13:05]
:CREATED_BY: johnw
:FINISHED: [2026-08-18 Tue 13:05]
:CLOSE_REASON: done
:SOURCE_REPO: agent-cat
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/src/agent-cat
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

Adopted from the 2026-08-14 web survey (comparison judge, acat-k28 phase C). (1) Add a second fold over the existing finite cost tree computing the selective-functors Over/Under pair: the UNDER set is the questions asked in every world (the floor — exactly the panel dispatchable immediately); the OVER set is every question any world can ask (the ceiling). No former, no shape change; sources: Mokhov et al Selective Applicative Functors (static.tex in github.com/snowleopard/selective), Haxl/ApplicativeDo as the cautionary tale (never recover the rung by a heuristic desugarer — GHC's own manual admits ApplicativeDo 'might miss an opportunity'; ours is syntax, {$x} vs {x}). (2) Presentation modeled on Snakemake --dry-run job tables + a --why column naming the binding that forced each question (Snakemake -r). (3) A machine-readable validate mode shaped like AWS ValidateStateMachineDefinition: result OK|FAIL plus diagnostics {code, message, severity, location}, pitched for CI/pre-commit, with the stated stability discipline (rely on result, not on message wording). (4) LlamaIndex framing for docs: the same declarations serve two moments — before a run (every possible path) and after (what happened); our cost tree IS the picture and a transcript is a sub-tree of it.

The applicative concurrent regression remains intact and passed 100/100 at -N8.

* DOING [#C] Complete the native Pi workflow client
:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       acat-8hq9
:LABELS:   []
:ISSUE_TYPE: feature
:CREATED:  [2026-09-09 Wed 00:08]
:MODIFIED: [2026-09-09 Wed 15:27]
:CREATED_BY: johnw
:SOURCE_REPO: agent-cat
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/src/agent-cat
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

Confirmed goal mttr7s7a-9dc4rh in fork/agent-cat. Extend ext-pi with multi-input native forms, exact-input consent and routing, output-first monitoring, local human answers, correlated controls, history/lineage, verified results, and deterministic real-Pi validation. Coordinate shared contracts and integration with Agent Deck session agent-cat (9ea419cf-1788468379). Preserve inherited uncommitted work. No paid workflow calls, deployment, staging, commits, or publication without separate authorization. Initial baseline and receipts: /tmp/agent-cat-pi-native.aK2KKu.

** Notes

Baseline and ownership established. Branch fork/agent-cat at 828e8cacb7ce37c0ffc01b8576562f8747a59868. Inherited patch, staged patch, 550 source identities, untracked source copies, and messaging receipts are private under /tmp/agent-cat-pi-native.aK2KKu. Installed Pi is 0.85.1, dependency pins are 0.84.3, node 22.23.2, npm 10.9.8, GHC 9.10.3. Fresh agentic-run built successfully. Type checks passed and baseline unit suite reports 89 passed, 5 skipped. Integration entry refuses at an obsolete RemoteSession.followUp prerequisite while the adapter uses the Chord service transport. This failure is being investigated rather than bypassed.

Capability matrix at baseline:
- Trusted catalogue, v1/v2/v3 descriptors, source-aware /wf parsing: present. Downstream wf registry reads 74 rows, but active global configuration names agentic-run. Test candidate will use isolated runner configuration.
- Multi-input form, field-file content loading, draft retention across native views: absent. Current inputs loop through individual editor prompts.
- Exact post-input review: incomplete. Legacy workflow approval precedes input collection in one path, and prepareLaunch regenerates plan/files. Shared final-input snapshot and explicit final review needed.
- Sanitized v2 persona/model routing: present, but complete consent/readiness and preserved policy identity need review.
- Structured event reduction, bounded public progress and diagnostics: present. Output-first native views, details, run browser and verified export UI needed.
- Local human answering: runtime and reducer support present. Pi launch fixes personAnswering=engine and RunHandle has no answerPerson method. Native question validation/control/UI required.
- Correlated cancel/steer/retry/recover/redirect: present. Integrate native affordances, ordered decisions and full stale/error coverage.
- Immutable restart/resume/fork and restore/owner heartbeat: present. Complete native browsing, final consent, human mode inheritance and cross-owner refusal coverage.
- Result artifacts: terminal verification present. Native reader/export and confinement/identity guarantees require further review.
- Current-session hook: installed Pi exposes startTaskTurn, published dependency type pin does not. Remote Chord transport exists but integration gate references obsolete API and a nonexistent test filename.

Coordination: agent-cat acknowledged ownership. Parent owns Emacs plus minimal shared runtime/CLI support and will not edit ext-pi. This fork owns ext-pi, and will not touch shared interfaces without agreement. Both retain descriptor-v3, protocol/control-v2, frontend-manifest-v2, existing human answering and exact plan/routing. Shared input-newline, approval identity, question-reference, artifact and cross-owner findings will be exchanged.

Native-input checkpoint: ext-pi/src/input-form.ts now replaces the sequential editor loop. It uses native Pi Editors/Input, one responsive multi-field form, multiline entry, Alt-e expansion, relative/absolute file loading, per-field drafts, exact unedited supplied/file values, a 1 MiB field bound, and terminal-control/UTF-8/file validation. Complete supplied inputs retain the existing direct path. New input-form tests and extension tests exercise the real component instead of returning fabricated form results.

Current-session IPC fixes: real Pi exposed Unix address overflow/truncation when the bridge socket was placed under a long durable state root. The bridge now uses a private short-lived directory with fixed socket/token leaf names, explicit OS byte limits and cleanup. Mode-0700 directory/mode-0600 token, exact socket existence, long-state startup and removal are tested. Another regression demonstrated that an unauthenticated connection could hang close. The bridge now bounds pending authentication, closes that peer at shutdown, handles peer errors locally, and stops parsing after malformed input. No new public protocol or shared Runtime/CLI source was introduced.

Owner directive (2026-08-15): find out whether ACP can talk to running, live sessions; if it can, add that infrastructure to agent-cat, so a workflow run can be observed interactively (the motivating case: an agent-deck session named agent-cat hosting the owner's interactive Claude, watching example/harden.wf run).

Facts already established from our side: agent-cat's ACP client speaks session/new only (Acp.newSession; no session/load anywhere); Adapter.ofName knows stub/claude/codex; --adapter-arg passes argv through verbatim; person questions already reach the operator's keyboard on live runs (Exec.Settings.askPersonOnStdin := !stubbed). A Claude session is owned by one process, so 'attach to the pane's session' may be impossible regardless of protocol support — the research must distinguish resume-into-new-process (fork?) from true live attach.

Research running (workflow wf_709221dd-563): (1) ACP spec semantics of session/load + loadSession capability + any non-stdio transport that reaches an already-running agent + multi-client/observer modes; (2) claude-agent-acp 0.66.0's loadSession behavior and resume/fork semantics under contention; (3) Claude Code/SDK resume semantics and any supported live-attach path.

Scope once research lands: at minimum, client-side session/load (capability-gated), a --session <id> flag on agent-cat run, and honest diagnoses for what cannot be attached; more if the protocol offers more.

Three layers, all confirmed:\n\n1. PROTOCOL. `session/load` is sequential handoff — the agent (a subprocess this client spawned) restores context and MUST replay the whole history as session/update notifications, then the client continues \"as if the session was never interrupted\". stdio-subprocess is the only defined transport (\"The client launches the agent as a subprocess\"; Streamable HTTP is a draft RFD). 1:1 client:session by assumption. The exact missing thing has an open RFD — \"Multi-Client Session Attach\" (PR #533, proposal stage since 2026-02: \"There is no mechanism for a second client to 'attach' to a live, in-progress session\"). Correction recorded: ACP versions are integers (current stable 1, which Acp.lean already sends); \"2025-11-25\" is our MCP revision, not ACP.\n\n2. ADAPTER (@agentclientprotocol/claude-agent-acp 0.66.0 — note the zed-industries name is deprecated). Advertises agentCapabilities.loadSession: true AND sessionCapabilities {additionalDirectories, close, delete, fork, list, resume}. session/load = SDK query with {resume: sessionId}, which CONTINUES IN PLACE (appends to the same session file; fork is a separate explicit path). No lock, no attach mode, always spawns its own claude binary; the entire argv surface is --cli/--version/--hide-claude-auth, so --adapter-arg buys nothing here.\n\n3. PRODUCT. \"A session started by running `claude` directly is tied to that terminal\" — not attachable. Live-attach exists only for opt-in surfaces: background sessions under the supervisor daemon (claude --bg; attach/logs/peek; wire protocol undocumented), Remote Control (Anthropic-mediated, no local API), the per-session messaging socket (inject-only, verified live here), channels (launch-time, research preview). agent-deck itself drives sessions by tmux send-keys (extracted from the installed binary).\n\nWHAT IS BUILDABLE AND WORTH IT (proposal, awaiting owner word):\n`agent-cat run --session <id>` via client-side session/load — sequential handoff. Motivating flow: exit the deck pane's claude, run the workflow with --session <that session's id> — it continues inside that transcript with all its context, person questions still arrive on the operator's keyboard — then `claude --resume <id>` afterwards shows the entire workflow in the session history. Implementation: capture agentCapabilities at initialize (refuse --session when loadSession is absent, naming the adapter), Conn.loadSession sending {sessionId, cwd, mcpServers: []} and draining replay updates until the response (accept null or object), CLI flag + a documented two-writers hazard (\"close the interactive owner first\" — continue-in-place has no lock, and agent-cat cannot detect a live writer). Optional follow-up: --fork-session via sessionCapabilities.fork for the context-without-mutation variant. * DONE [#C] Engine: agent-deck as a payload transport beside the ACP adapters — send/output into live sessions
:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       acat-agent-deck-engine-vv6
:LABELS:   []
:ISSUE_TYPE: feature
:CREATED:  [2026-08-15 Sat 22:16]
:MODIFIED: [2026-08-17 Mon 19:52]
:CREATED_BY: johnw
:FINISHED: [2026-08-17 Mon 19:52]
:CLOSE_REASON: done
:SOURCE_REPO: agent-cat
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/src/agent-cat
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

Owner directive (2026-08-15): add agent-deck as another ENGINE for delivering and working with payloads, alongside claude and codex — and able to talk to claude and codex via agent-deck.

Why this matters beyond parity: it is the answer to the live-session question acat-acp-live-session-99n closed negatively. ACP cannot reach a live session at any layer; agent-deck can — `agent-deck session send <id> <message>` delivers into any running pane (Claude, Codex, Pi, OpenCode) and `session output` polls the last response, sidestepping the single-writer rollout hazard entirely. A deck engine therefore gives `agent-cat run` the interactive-observation mode the owner originally asked for: the workflow's questions land in a live, watched pane, and answers come back from it.

Scope sketch (agent-cat side):
- A new engine beside the ACP adapters, with a different transport: not stdio ACP but the agent-deck CLI — send a rendered prompt via `agent-deck session send`, poll `session output` for the reply, decode with the existing per-kind Decode. Turn boundaries and timeouts need design (deck output is text, not structured stop reasons); `session approve` maps to the permission axis for codex targets.
- CLI surface: something like `agent-cat run program.wf --engine deck --session <deck-session>`; person questions can stay on the operator's stdin, or be routed into the same pane — design choice.
- The primitives to build on, verified in the owner's probe notes: send, output, approve, fork, handoff, parent/child links, -assert-done sentinels, transition notifications, codex-hooks/gemini-hooks.
- Safety rules to encode, from the owner's architecture note: never ACP-loadSession a thread whose TUI is live (single writer per rollout) — the deck's send is the safe path to a live session; one writer per worktree.
- Relation to the three-planes doctrine (repo = data plane, deck = control plane, ACP = embedding layer for headless/IDE): the deck engine is agent-cat's control-plane citizenship; the ACP adapters remain the headless path, and the parked --session/loadSession feature (99n) remains the headless-handoff path.

The owner's full architecture note is preserved below for the design round.
---
[Three planes: 1. repository as data plane (AGENTS.md + CLAUDE.md symlink, .agents/skills, obr/PLAN.org as vendor-neutral ledger; doctrine: work is handed off via issues and commits, chat channels only signal). 2. agent-deck as control plane (session send/output/approve/fork/handoff, parent-child links, -assert-done, transition notifications, codex-hooks/gemini-hooks; launch through agent-deck launch; name decks after lanes; use send/output/handoff as the inter-vendor mailbox instead of raw ACP). 3. ACP adapters as embedding layer (IDE hosting and headless programmatic control; Claude-hosted ACP sessions appear in Claude's peer registry, codex's do not; Claude↔Claude native, Claude↔codex via deck (live) or ACP session/new-loadSession (headless); never loadSession a live codex thread. Roles: Claude Code orchestrator/reviewer; Codex heads-down implementer in isolated worktree; Pi third opinion; one writer per worktree; checkpoint self-sync (obr sync --import-only + deck messages + labels) in AGENTS.md; Nix hygiene: pin adapter+CLI pairs together via CLAUDE_CODE_EXECUTABLE/CODEX_PATH.]

V4 (wave-2 fess) predicted and wave-3 close-out reproduced it: under machine load (loadavg ~11) the oracle's cold spawn can exceed bisim's 60s ping timeout, producing a phantom transport failure ('the oracle is wedged') on a healthy tree; the immediate direct ping answered instantly and the rerun was clean. Fix: one retry of the initial ping (or a spawn-aware first-ping budget) before declaring the wedge. Evidence on acat-isaac-decisions-vtt.

* DONE [#C] DSL diagnostics: EOF at 0:0, positionless Chunk, arm-before-scrutinee ordering, misleading messages
:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       acat-bqx
:LABELS:   []
:ISSUE_TYPE: task
:CREATED:  [2026-08-14 Fri 19:00]
:MODIFIED: [2026-08-20 Thu 10:11]
:CREATED_BY: johnw
:FINISHED: [2026-08-20 Thu 10:11]
:CLOSE_REASON: done
:SOURCE_REPO: agent-cat
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/src/agent-cat
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

deep-review + alexey + validated. (1) End-of-input errors report 0:0 (Parse.lean:174-181), violating the one-based Pos contract; cli/AgentCat.lean:224-225 then drops the caret entirely — the commonest editing error (truncated file) gets the worst message. Fix: carry the end-of-source position. (2) Chunk.interp has no Pos (Syntax.lean:118-123), so every prompt error is reported at the enclosing ask (Check.lean:112-151) — in a language whose principal dataflow mechanism is interpolation. Fix: add Pos to Chunk.interp (the lexer knows it, Parse.lean:117-127). (3) ifFlag/caseVerdict elaborate all arms before checking the scrutinee's kind (Check.lean:392-426), so a wrong-kinded scrutinee reports an error from inside an arm. Fix: test bnd.at? first. (4) Panel monoid refusal names the first member's kind as everyone's (Check.lean:250-258). (5) Interpolation names lex as any run up to }/"/newline (Parse.lean:118), so non-identifiers surface later as 'unbound name' at the wrong place. (6) `up to 2 revision` and `up to 1 revisions` both parse (Parse.lean:430-435), defeating the two spellings' stated purpose.

The tests-vs-theorems analysis (comment on acat-k28 pending) found ~10 cheap, compile-verified theorems whose content the battery checks only at fixtures. Add them where their subjects live:

- checkProgram_overRevised / checkProgram_oversized / checkProgram_of_within — the program front end's three guards fire on exactly their conditions, at the pinned position/excerpt/count, for every program (one-unfold proofs).
- overRevised_sound — a bound the pre-scan reports really exceeds maxRevisions (structural induction, ~28 lines).
- check_panel_nil — an empty panel is refused with the exact diagnosis over every Γ, S, annotation and position, through Dsl.check (must live in Check.lean; freshName is private).
- checkArgs_too_few / checkArgs_too_many — the two arity refusals, kept distinct, message pinned, by rfl.
- checkBlock_caseVerdict_arms — a checked case lands approve/object/declined on the three DISTINCT checked arms (this refutes the battery's own comment that 'no theorem constrains the VTag mapping'; update that comment).
- Verdict.tag_object — nonempty objections tag as .object.
- askShape_draw + bindForm_ask_head_draw — the source-written draw index reaches the question record, for every ask.
- expand_lit / expand_interp_hit / expand_append — the define expansion is a monoid map hitting the found body (must live in Parse.lean; expand is private).

Also the test-robustness repairs the same analysis surfaced: three assertions in test/DslSmoke.lean pass vacuously when evsOf swallows a check failure into [] (the two evs.all checks and the CRLF decide-equality); guard each with a nonemptiness conjunct. And §10g throws instead of failing a named check when run outside the repo root.

Discipline: mutation testing showed the theorems complement the string pins (a ∀-theorem survives swapped diagnoses that the pins catch), so nothing is deleted except where a theorem strictly subsumes a hand-built fixture.

Found by the connection-research attack pass (acat-lean-haskell-connection-lhi): the parser's freshOfTables refuses a function name that spells an earlier function, but checkFnsList (Check.lean) never checks the accumulating table for a duplicate name, and Fns.find? / fnSigsOf are first-wins list lookups — so a hand-built RawProgram with two  entries (different arity or different bodies) checks, with every call silently resolving to the FIRST and bodyAsks pricing against it. Same shape as the served-by gap (acat-served-by-check-gap-i5d, fixed): mirror the parser's refusal in checkFnsList with the existing diagnosis wording, one hand-built battery case in the check_panel_nil style, audit the new site.

* DONE [#B] Engine: party→backend routing, so one run can span adapters (PAL-consensus parity)
:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       acat-engine-party-routing-hcx
:LABELS:   []
:ISSUE_TYPE: feature
:CREATED:  [2026-08-19 Wed 12:17]
:MODIFIED: [2026-08-19 Wed 19:30]
:CREATED_BY: johnw
:FINISHED: [2026-08-19 Wed 19:30]
:CLOSE_REASON: done
:SOURCE_REPO: agent-cat
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/src/agent-cat
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

Today both live engines bind EVERY addressee to one backend per run — run/Main.hs prints it as the contract: 'every addressee — model, tool and person — is this one adapter/session' (:590, :622). The Plan semantics already distinguish parties and servedBy alternates already record who answered, so a cross-model panel (gpt-5.5-pro AND gemini-3.1-pro judging the same patch in ONE run) is expressible but not executable. This is the single capability PAL MCP has that agent-cat cannot yet execute (doc/research/pal-vs-agent-cat.md). Design sketch: a party→backend routing table on the run (e.g. --route 'reviewer-secure=acp:codex' --route 'reviewer-simple=deck:PANE', default route for the rest), engine header naming every route, trace scope unchanged (it already records the answering party). Fail-over alternates then become cross-backend for free. With this landed, PAL MCP is fully subsumed by workflows + engine config.

** Notes

Confirmed architecture rulings
- `dsl` is strictly runtime-independent.
- An optional, engine-neutral `runtime` API may expose run/session/routing facts; importing it is an explicit purity opt-out.
- Shipped workflow packages remain DSL-only; runtime-aware probes belong to runtime/CLI tests.
- `bisim` may have test-only dependencies on `cost`, `workflow/core`, and `workflow/example` so it checks the exact analyzer and executable workflows rather than copies.
- `route-live.sh` is preserved but excluded from automatic verification because it performs paid calls.

- ext-pi: TypeScript check; 67 unit tests passed with five skipped; 10 integration tests passed.

Ownership map before moves
- model: Lean mathematical/proof sources and Pollution test.
- bisim: Lean conformance/oracle/generator, corpus, Haskell Gen/Observe/Oracle/world fixtures and tier0/tier1/live differential.

* DONE [#D] DSL reference page: elaboration contract, three named hazards, determinism law, edit-preservation statement
:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       acat-f8e
:LABELS:   []
:ISSUE_TYPE: task
:CREATED:  [2026-08-14 Fri 20:22]
:MODIFIED: [2026-08-27 Thu 15:09]
:CREATED_BY: johnw
:FINISHED: [2026-08-27 Thu 15:09]
:CLOSE_REASON: Covered by doc/agent-cat.texi: checker contract, hazards, determinism, edit-preservation, extension rules, round identity, and panel semantics are documented against the live Haskell/Lean spine.
:SOURCE_REPO: agent-cat
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/src/agent-cat
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

Adopted from the web survey (comparison judge). (1) CONTRACT: name the checker's five refusal clauses as an advertised validation contract (Microsoft Agent Framework precedent): every hole names a live binder or earlier define of the right kind; every branch scrutinee is flag/verdict with all arms written in order; every binder's kind matches its source; no live name introduced twice and no binder spells a define; known-here matches Gamma. (2) HAZARDS, named not fixed: (a) panel annihilation — a declined member zeroes objections so {why.reasons} is legitimately empty (Inspect AI's 'collect' reducer is the shape of an eventual monoid-side fix; see acat-88m); (b) arms export nothing — a binding inside a branch arm cannot reach the join, a PRINCIPLED refusal (a value exported from an arm has a sum type and Ctx holds Codes); (c) the kind is the shape, the spec define is a courtesy — flag is what the adapter must satisfy, flagSpec is words helping the addressee produce it, and they can drift (PDL derives the constraint from the type; that is an adapter-layer fix). (3) DETERMINISM AS LAW: given the same answer sheet the run is the same — free here since meaning is a function on total sheets; MAF needs a Pregel barrier to approximate it; say it out loud because the field taught users nondeterminism. (4) EDIT-PRESERVATION, honest form: editing a question's WORDS changes its index into Omega (answers not preserved); what IS preserved is the shape sequence and the cost tree — the user-visible payoff of shape-as-data and what define buys (editing verdictSpec moves no binding and no cost node). (5) Two review criteria as standing rules (YAWL OR-join cautionary tale): no former's enablement may depend on state outside its own subterm; a construct whose cost is not readable from its own syntax must show it in the notation. (6) One-sentence docs items: per-iteration questions are distinct by unrolling (memoization does not collapse rounds); 'all must approve' is the named-reducer pattern at set size one and the comma slot is reserved for the day a second verdict combiner exists (PoLL evidence).

* TODO [#C] Runtime follow-ups: completed-turn discipline for ack/text decodes; stopReason on person-addressed turns; Settings.log unused
:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       acat-fuk
:LABELS:   []
:ISSUE_TYPE: task
:CREATED:  [2026-08-13 Thu 02:06]
:MODIFIED: [2026-08-20 Thu 10:12]
:CREATED_BY: johnw
:SOURCE_REPO: agent-cat
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/src/agent-cat
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

From the runtime adversarial pass (commit 71a212a). MEDIUM: (1) Exec.say logs but proceeds on stopReason != end_turn, so a cancelled/refused ACP turn decodes as '' on .text and () on .ack — refusal-is-an-answer is a documented decode position, but an ACT acknowledgement arguably requires a COMPLETED turn; decide per-code turn-completion requirements and enforce in Exec.say. (2) The .person branch uses conn.prompt which discards Turn.stopReason entirely (Acp.lean:376) — same issue, person-addressed. LOW: (3) nothing consults Settings.log in the demo harness, so warnings are invisible in exit-0 runs — either surface warnings in harden_demo output or drop the setting. Context: the HIGH fabrication finding was already fixed (exhaustion aborts); these are the nearest remaining places where a failed turn and an answered turn leave the same cell.

Frozen plan: doc/research/workflow-manager-implementation-plan.md, Section 2.
Plan SHA-256: 80d0fc1f5785d1ba18136c840492f83d8920ea33d67e609c44f098f5d7c5ebf6

| G0: implementation baseline | Exact source/toolchain provenance, owner agreement, compatibility fixtures, package/import rules, and the accepted API/state contract are recorded. | WM-001 through WM-007. |

This is an independent evidence gate, not another implementation. Check the actual integrated source, theorem/axiom output, applicable deterministic/failure/platform/client tests, and compatibility. Do not close merely because prerequisite issues are closed. Preserve all stated evidence ceilings. No deployment or publication is authorized by this gate.

Global invariants:
| I01 | Agent-cat is the sole workflow interpreter. The manager does not compute answers, bills, memoization, routing decisions, or authored traces independently. | WM-004, WM-005, WM-015, WM-022, WM-040. |
| I02 | Requests, admission, live preparations, runtime status, supervision, pending decisions, and result verification are separate dimensions. | WM-004, WM-009, WM-013 through WM-018. |
| I03 | Only the exact live prepared worker can consume an approval. Changed inputs, profile, worker, or process generation require fresh preparation and consent. | WM-012, WM-014, WM-020, WM-027. |
| I04 | A manager-owned control pipe outlives any HTTP/SSE connection. Network disconnection does not become control EOF. | WM-012, WM-019, WM-026, WM-039. |
| I05 | Durable acceptance, attempted delivery, runtime acknowledgement, control effect, and terminal outcome are distinct facts. Ambiguous effects are not automatically replayed. | WM-010, WM-014 through WM-016, WM-020, WM-027. |
| I06 | Mandatory decisions use one per-run FIFO reservation mechanism across every endpoint. Controls retain exact occurrence, attempt, and correlation identity. | WM-016, WM-027, WM-039. |
| I07 | Stored paths, root identities, native IDs, PIDs, and invocation metadata do not confer execution or signalling authority. | WM-008, WM-012, WM-018 through WM-020, WM-024. |
| I08 | Initial inputs retain their literal/captured representation and byte contract. Typed runtime answers retain false, null, structured values, and runtime decoding. | WM-005, WM-011, WM-016, WM-029 through WM-038. |
| I09 | Artifacts are verified through retained trusted references before any successful content response. Export remains exclusive and confined. | WM-007, WM-017, WM-018, WM-025, WM-027. |
| I10 | Coordination state and invalidations commit together. Runtime sequence validation remains independent of manager commit order. | WM-009, WM-010, WM-015, WM-025, WM-026. |
| I11 | Replay has an explicit retained prefix. Snapshot boundaries, open-stream eviction, duplicate handling, and client refresh ordering cannot silently lose state. | WM-021, WM-025, WM-026, WM-029, WM-039. |
| I12 | Every application operation is authenticated and authorized. The only unauthenticated CORS exception is a bounded non-resource preflight check. | WM-023, WM-024, WM-027, WM-028. |
| I13 | Credential rotation preserves the registered client's mutation ledger. Backup restoration invalidates old authority and never treats lost receipts as unexecuted work. | WM-010, WM-020, WM-023, WM-028. |
| I14 | Resource bounds are enforced before large allocation and at global as well as client/profile boundaries. Safety supervision survives ordinary queue saturation. | WM-007, WM-009 through WM-013, WM-019, WM-021, WM-024 through WM-028. |
| I15 | Existing descriptor, session, protocol, control, manifest, store, and CLI branches retain their meaning. Client local mode does not silently become service mode. | WM-003, WM-005, WM-006, WM-018, WM-029 through WM-038. |
| I16 | Mathematical proofs, deterministic tests, platform tests, cross-machine tests, and deployment evidence have separate stated ceilings. | WM-004, WM-022, WM-039 through WM-044. |

** Acceptance Criteria

Exact source/toolchain provenance, owner agreement, compatibility fixtures, package/import rules, and the accepted API/state contract are recorded. Actual evidence and independent verification are recorded. No unavailable check is reported as green.

** Notes

Operator-authorized scope amendment 2026-09-12T06:03:20+00:00: user explicitly instructed, "Skip this test, drop the requirement. I want to continue." The unrun SQLite directory-replacement confinement experiment and guarantee are withdrawn, not passed. Amendment doc/research/workflow-manager-storage-amendment.md, SHA-256 e6654b8ec1294e1daf2bb634ce3da62426cdb36fdcb9cb820a54ffc192c5e791, supersedes only D2/WM-007 and their dependent acceptance obligation, preserving both original research hashes. Ordinary SQLite work now assumes a stable operator-controlled private local namespace. No existing guard or runnable regression is removed, no rejected experiment is repeated, and no custom VFS is authorized. The already independently reviewed durable publication work satisfies the remaining WM-007 scope. Fresh warning-fatal all-target baseline and all three Haskell suites pass in state.0wnll6Qx/baseline.log. WM-009 may proceed under the amendment. G0 remains open until independently assessed. Full WM-001–044/G0–G5 remains the objective. Refocus completed 2026-09-12T06:03:20+00:00, next deadline 2026-09-12T07:03:20+00:00. Next: publish this scope amendment and implement ordinary coordination storage without reconstructing the rejected experiment.

G0 accepted under the explicit storage amendment at 2026-09-12T06:35:51+00:00. Independent fresh-origin reviewer85a0ee89-3113-45d9-a524-cec6541cd314 found no implementation defect and one missing minimum-client-environment record. Parent declared the support minima in manager/README.md, separated policy from observed/tested versions and future client acceptance, and retained all native peer ranges. Same evaluator85c2d48a-5aa7-49f7-87b7-997e56ef10c7 completed the gate assessment as Ready with no further prerequisite. Evidence: state.0wnll6Qx/g0-review.md, g0-final-review.md, g0-source.json, g0-policy-source.json, baseline.log, amendment-doc-check.log, client-baseline-doc-check.log, client-baseline-source.log and verified history-probe artifacts. All recorded source matches the assessed snapshot except the documented README policy addition. Original design/plan hashes, fixtures, code, model and dependencies are unchanged. The SQLite directory-replacement experiment remains unrun and its withdrawn guarantee is not certified. G0 establishes the baseline/API/model/ownership boundary only, not a service, physical power-loss, containment, approved worker, SQL realization, cross-client, provider or deployment capability.

| I10 | Coordination state and invalidations commit together. Runtime sequence validation remains independent of manager commit order. | WM-009, WM-010, WM-015, WM-025, WM-026. |
| I11 | Replay has an explicit retained prefix. Snapshot boundaries, open-stream eviction, duplicate handling, and client refresh ordering cannot silently lose state. | WM-021, WM-025, WM-026, WM-029, WM-039. |
| I12 | Every application operation is authenticated and authorized. The only unauthenticated CORS exception is a bounded non-resource preflight check. | WM-023, WM-024, WM-027, WM-028. |
| I13 | Credential rotation preserves the registered client's mutation ledger. Backup restoration invalidates old authority and never treats lost receipts as unexecuted work. | WM-010, WM-020, WM-023, WM-028. |
| I14 | Resource bounds are enforced before large allocation and at global as well as client/profile boundaries. Safety supervision survives ordinary queue saturation. | WM-007, WM-009 through WM-013, WM-019, WM-021, WM-024 through WM-028. |
| I15 | Existing descriptor, session, protocol, control, manifest, store, and CLI branches retain their meaning. Client local mode does not silently become service mode. | WM-003, WM-005, WM-006, WM-018, WM-029 through WM-038. |
| I16 | Mathematical proofs, deterministic tests, platform tests, cross-machine tests, and deployment evidence have separate stated ceilings. | WM-004, WM-022, WM-039 through WM-044. |

** Acceptance Criteria

Protected HTTP access exposes authorized catalogue, history, snapshots, verified content, SSE, and polling, with bounded parsing and revocable credentials. Mutation routes remain unavailable. Actual evidence and independent verification are recorded. No unavailable check is reported as green.

| G5: release candidate | Full failure/capacity evidence, operational procedures, reproducible packages, rollback, documentation, and independent review are complete. Deployment remains an explicit operator action. | G3, G4, and WM-041 through WM-044. |

This is an independent evidence gate, not another implementation. Check the actual integrated source, theorem/axiom output, applicable deterministic/failure/platform/client tests, and compatibility. Do not close merely because prerequisite issues are closed. Preserve all stated evidence ceilings. No deployment or publication is authorized by this gate.

Global invariants:
| I01 | Agent-cat is the sole workflow interpreter. The manager does not compute answers, bills, memoization, routing decisions, or authored traces independently. | WM-004, WM-005, WM-015, WM-022, WM-040. |
| I02 | Requests, admission, live preparations, runtime status, supervision, pending decisions, and result verification are separate dimensions. | WM-004, WM-009, WM-013 through WM-018. |

| I09 | Artifacts are verified through retained trusted references before any successful content response. Export remains exclusive and confined. | WM-007, WM-017, WM-018, WM-025, WM-027. |

| I11 | Replay has an explicit retained prefix. Snapshot boundaries, open-stream eviction, duplicate handling, and client refresh ordering cannot silently lose state. | WM-021, WM-025, WM-026, WM-029, WM-039. |
| I12 | Every application operation is authenticated and authorized. The only unauthenticated CORS exception is a bounded non-resource preflight check. | WM-023, WM-024, WM-027, WM-028. |
| I13 | Credential rotation preserves the registered client's mutation ledger. Backup restoration invalidates old authority and never treats lost receipts as unexecuted work. | WM-010, WM-020, WM-023, WM-028. |
| I14 | Resource bounds are enforced before large allocation and at global as well as client/profile boundaries. Safety supervision survives ordinary queue saturation. | WM-007, WM-009 through WM-013, WM-019, WM-021, WM-024 through WM-028. |
| I15 | Existing descriptor, session, protocol, control, manifest, store, and CLI branches retain their meaning. Client local mode does not silently become service mode. | WM-003, WM-005, WM-006, WM-018, WM-029 through WM-038. |
| I16 | Mathematical proofs, deterministic tests, platform tests, cross-machine tests, and deployment evidence have separate stated ceilings. | WM-004, WM-022, WM-039 through WM-044. |

Final acceptance matrix:
| A01 | Create a request with several missing inputs. Empty text is present, null-as-text refuses, duplicate/unknown names refuse, and readiness creates no engine work. | WM-011, then WM-027 and all client gates. |
| A02 | Upload Unicode/CRLF/trailing-LF bytes, mutate the original file, and prepare. Captured digest and resulting semantic input remain exact. Exercise inline JSON expansion and the server-owned large-file path. | WM-005, WM-011, WM-022, then WM-028. |
| A03 | Enqueue competing shared/independent profiles at maximum reservations. Confirm FIFO eligibility, no partial-resource deadlock, review occupancy, and cleanup-based release. | WM-013, WM-019, WM-041. |
| A04 | Edit/expire/revoke a preparation or kill its worker before approval. An old digest/ETag cannot start a replacement worker. Restart requires a new review. | WM-014, WM-020, then WM-028. |
| A05 | Commit start intent and lose the HTTP reply or kill the manager around pipe delivery. Exact retries return the same receipt and no second start is attempted. Ambiguity remains explicit. | WM-010, WM-014, WM-020, WM-028. |
| A06 | Run two workflows with active attempts and mandatory decisions simultaneously. Show both dimensions, choose another run, and reject a non-head answer through every endpoint. | WM-015, WM-016, WM-027, WM-039. |
| A07 | Race two clients answering the same generation with false/null/structured data. One reservation wins, typed bytes remain correct, stale/unsupported/failed delivery is distinguished, and timeout does not allow a second answer. | WM-016, WM-028, WM-039. |
| A08 | Exercise retry, failover, abandon, redirect, both steering timings, and cancellation. Exact control IDs and occurrence/attempt effects correlate, including `occurrence.retried`. | WM-016, all native UI gates. |
| A09 | Close all network clients during active work and outstanding decisions. Work remains owned, decisions remain pending, and another authorized client can reconnect. | WM-012, WM-026, WM-039. |
| A10 | Send duplicate/gapped/wrong-run/conflicting envelopes and terminal events without valid authored trace. Preserve valid state and refuse invalid transitions without manufactured success. | WM-006, WM-015, WM-022, WM-040. |
| A11 | Report runtime success but remove/corrupt its result. Show terminal status separately from unavailable content. Verify a known good artifact despite journal corruption. | WM-017, WM-025, WM-028. |
| A12 | Replace state/export roots and race identical export names. At most one complete exclusive publication wins, existing bytes remain unchanged, and lost receipts never cause overwrite. | WM-007, WM-017, WM-028. |
| A13 | Restart/resume/fork legacy/v2/v3 parents with typed drop/replacement edits. Preserve parent facts and refuse invocation mismatch, live ownership, or quarantined resources. | WM-018, WM-020, WM-022, WM-040. |
| A14 | Attach SSE at a snapshot boundary while commits occur. Reconnect after a partial block, evict unseen events during an open stream, and expire detail pages. Apply complete events or resnapshot without gaps. | WM-025, WM-026, WM-028, WM-039. |
| A15 | Delay GET responses and resnapshot/switch endpoints while they are pending. Old responses/page sets cannot overwrite a newer fetch generation. Opaque ETags are never numerically sorted. | WM-029, WM-030, WM-033, WM-036. |
| A16 | Revoke/rotate credentials during POST, artifact download, pagination, and SSE. Preserve registered-client idempotency across rotation, revoke ongoing access, and fence later intents. | WM-023, WM-028, WM-039. |
| A17 | Restore an older backup after accepted operations and revocations. Old credentials, cursors, and authority keys fail. No unresolved old operation is reissued under a new key automatically. | WM-020, WM-028, WM-039. |
| A18 | Send allowed/forbidden CORS preflights, missing/forged Origin, unexpected Host, bypassed proxy headers, and redirects. Policy-only preflight reveals no resource, and actual access remains authenticated. | WM-024, WM-028. |
| A19 | Saturate uploads/drafts/event streams/page sets/commands and fill the DB disk. Refuse normal work honestly, keep bounded safety supervision available, and never acknowledge an uncommitted cancellation. | WM-019, WM-021, WM-028, WM-041. |
| A20 | Kill manager/worker/group leader and exercise escaped descendants on Linux and macOS. Prove containment or quarantine affected resources, never signal a recycled stored PID. | WM-019, WM-020, WM-041, WM-043. |
| A21 | Inject synthetic tokens, prompts, answers, provider diagnostics, HTML, and terminal escapes into failure paths. Incidental logs/URLs/argv remain clean and displays remain safe. | WM-008, WM-017, WM-023, WM-028, native UI gates. |
| A22 | Perform real keyboard interaction at three terminal sizes, preserving setup/review/answer/control drafts across resize and reconnect. Terminal/windows/focus are restored. | WM-032, WM-035, WM-038. |
| A23 | Create in one client, approve/answer in another, observe in a third across a real machine boundary, then disconnect/reconnect and verify lineage/results. | WM-039. |
| A24 | Upgrade and roll back a manager candidate with pending and completed work. Preserve compatible read-only history, reject incompatible DB versions, and retain explicit local client mode without live ownership transfer. Run local startup/pruning against distinct fixture roots and prove that manager records, lineage parents, and partial directories remain untouched. | WM-008, WM-020, WM-038, WM-042, WM-043. |

** Acceptance Criteria

Full failure/capacity evidence, operational procedures, reproducible packages, rollback, documentation, and independent review are complete. Deployment remains an explicit operator action. Actual evidence and independent verification are recorded. No unavailable check is reported as green.

* DONE [#B] The Haskell program: typed workflow library + evaluator/runner, Lean retained as the proven specification
:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       acat-haskell-program-xgw
:LABELS:   []
:ISSUE_TYPE: epic

:CREATED_BY: johnw
:FINISHED: [2026-08-17 Mon 01:48]

:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

Program of record per the owner's resolution of acat-haskell-surface-ygy (2026-08-16). Haskell is the production implementation: the typed workflow library of haskell-question.md Part 1 (the attacked design — first-order Tmpl prompts via quasi-quoter, variable tokens not answers, Codensity-bindable helpers, Discardable statement discipline, Checked-consuming runPlan, positional verdict arms, lazy-stream budgets turning recursive builders into refusals) as the authoring surface, plus the evaluator/runner (backends, content-addressed leaf store with fork/resume, typed backend/model pairing, preflight — the §6.3 invariants: acts never cacheable, hits are Cached never Done, keys salted with backend+model, verify runs checks itself vs agentVerify trusting a claim). The Lean formalization is retained in full as the mathematical anchor (meaning = Ω → (A × Trace), proven); no .wf in production (surface frozen at round 16; r17/r18 pages remain design records with reopening conditions).

Phases:
1. CONNECTION RESEARCH (first gate): the four mechanisms — bespoke Lean→Haskell extractor for this first-order fragment; FFI-linking the Lean-compiled C core; Lean as a subprocess semantic engine; reimplementation + bisimulation — 2026 tooling reality, real costs, recommendation to the owner.
2. MOCK AGENT + BISIMULATION HARNESS (built regardless of 1): a shared scripted world both sides operate against; property tests (generated programs and worlds) comparing traces and bills between Lean's denote/trace and the Haskell evaluator; the harness is the permanent conformance boundary.
3. THE LIBRARY per Part 1, amended by phase 1's outcome (if extraction/FFI wins, the semantic core is generated/linked rather than written).
4. THE RUNNER (the shared floor + the store).
5. The serialized checked-term format for the MCP/agent-authoring surface (the prose surface stops being .wf; the replacement is the serialized first-order format checked by checkPlan — priced in the report).

The Lean-side queue (99n --session, then vv6 deck engine) proceeds against the existing agent-cat CLI as spec/test runner; their production equivalents land in phase 4.

* DONE [#B] Strategic: continue the .wf DSL, or make Haskell the surface (agent-functor-style QQ + typed workflow library + verified evaluator)?
:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       acat-haskell-surface-ygy
:LABELS:   []
:ISSUE_TYPE: question
:CREATED:  [2026-08-16 Sun 10:38]
:MODIFIED: [2026-08-16 Sun 18:38]
:CREATED_BY: johnw
:FINISHED: [2026-08-16 Sun 18:02]
:CLOSE_REASON: Answered by the owner: option (e) — Haskell operational implementation + retained Lean formalization + bisimulation/extraction connection; see final comment
:SOURCE_REPO: agent-cat
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/src/agent-cat
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

Owner question (2026-08-16): the round-17/18 surface converges on Haskell. What would a Haskell library look like that offers a type for constructing these workflows plus a verified evaluator against a backend agent? Would it look like agent-functor (~/src/agent-functor: quasi-quoter for interpolating large strings into expression arguments), or can that design be improved? Deliverable: a deep-dive report (workflow: agent-functor autopsy + agent-cat guarantees inventory + prior-art sweep -> best-possible Haskell design + three-way comparison -> adversarial pass) informing whether to (a) continue the DSL project (round-18 campaign acat-r18-campaign-2s9 is gated and waiting), (b) switch to Haskell as surface syntax + typing discipline with QQ prompts, or (c) a hybrid (Haskell EDSL frontend elaborating to the Lean-verified kernel). Key theory to weigh: Applicative/Selective/Monad maps onto batch/branch/dynamic rungs (Mokhov's selective functors; Build Systems a la Carte's applicative-vs-monadic tasks = the level lattice); pipeline (static shape, value-dependent prompts) fits none of the classes directly and is where the Lean formers earn their keep; Haskell cannot restrict >>='s continuation, so Monad = dyn; 'verified evaluator' in pure Haskell is property-tested at best, so the honest options are Liquid Haskell, extraction pairings, or keeping Lean as the kernel. The recommendation is the owner's decision; the report must be honest about what each path loses.

(4) Pro-Haskell note kept: .wf questions reached through calls can never be closed (params are Exprs, fn-import-attack A3), while Haskell signatures restore closedness — the one place the embedding strictly beats the landed design. :LABELS:   []

:MODIFIED: [2026-08-16 Sun 21:38]
:CREATED_BY: johnw
:FINISHED: [2026-08-16 Sun 21:38]
:CLOSE_REASON: Fixed: replayArrivals first-occurrence comparison; McpSmoke 2b pins the memo-hit run
:SOURCE_REPO: agent-cat
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/src/agent-cat
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

Found by the connection-research attack pass (acat-lean-haskell-connection-lhi): heardMatchesReplay is decide (r.trace = rep.transcript) where r.trace = r.heard.map (.event) — deliver appends one Heard per SURFACED question and Dlg.resume walks past questions the table already answers, so arrivals are memo-deduped — while rep.transcript = Plan.trace (worldOf table) p Env.nil replays EVERY event including repeats. On a run with any repeated question (billMemo < billFresh), the check is an assertion of falsehood. Repair options: compare heard against the dedup of the replay (respecting Lean dedup keeping the LAST occurrence), or record the intended invariant differently. Add an McpSmoke case with a repeated question to pin whichever is chosen.

Pi integration identified two missing shared contracts. Workflow descriptor v3 does not advertise frontend sessions, history queries, verified artifact export, or accepted input-source variants. Add explicit machine-readable discovery without inferring these capabilities from descriptor versions or scraping help. Native supervisor manifests currently use the registry binary name and getExecutablePath. They neither accept nor retain configured client aliases or trusted wrapper executable/prefixArgs identity. Agree a bounded, non-secret invocation record that survives preparation and lineage, remains distinct from server identity, and is matched against current trusted client configuration on restoration rather than executed from stored data. Preserve existing descriptor/protocol/manifest compatibility and agree wire changes with the ext-pi owner before implementation. Shared source remains owned by session 9ea419cf-1788468379.

* DONE [#B] Resolve bounded 0 vs static: scale is not monotone in its body
:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       acat-l59
:LABELS:   []
:ISSUE_TYPE: bug
:CREATED:  [2026-08-11 Tue 20:10]
:MODIFIED: [2026-08-11 Tue 23:58]
:CREATED_BY: johnw
:FINISHED: [2026-08-11 Tue 23:58]
:CLOSE_REASON: scale n (bounded m) = bounded (n * max 1 m) — the static-shell arithmetic, as decided: a fan instantiates its whole body including its static shell, so the multiplier never drops below 1. Monotonicity restored in BOTH arguments (scale_le_scale_right was the false direction, now a 9-case proof); scale n static = scale n (bounded 0) agree (scale_bounded_zero); the under-graded witness memorialized in Term.lean at its honest grade bounded 3 with the old wrong grade named; residual static-vs-bounded-0 slack machine-checked and documented as upper-bound-safe; the old rationalizing paragraph deleted
:SOURCE_REPO: agent-cat
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/src/agent-cat
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

static <= bounded 0 in the order, but scale n static = bounded n while scale n (bounded 0) = bounded 0 — under the fixed payload reading these should agree. Candidates: normalize bounded 0 to static (costs definitional reductions) or scale n (bounded m) = bounded (n * max 1 m). Until decided, no fold may assume scale monotone in its body.

* DONE [#B] Connection research: extractor vs FFI vs subprocess vs bisimulation — how the Haskell program meets the Lean proofs

:LABELS:   []

:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

Phase 1 gate of acat-haskell-program-xgw. Research the four mechanisms with 2026 tooling reality and real costs: (i) a bespoke Lean metaprogram emitting Haskell for exactly this codebase's first-order core (Plan/denote/Dlg/decode are small structural recursions over inductives — feasible? auditable? maintenance cost as the core moves?); (ii) FFI: link the Lean-compiled C core into the Haskell runner via Lean's C API (the verified artifact itself executes; runtime init, boxed-object marshalling, build-system reality under Nix); (iii) Lean as a subprocess semantic engine the Haskell shell steps per question (zero reimplementation; latency trivial next to an LLM call; process-lifecycle and serialization costs); (iv) reimplementation + bisimulation property tests (the baseline — two implementations forever, drift caught only as often as the generators are good). Deliverable: recommendation to the owner with honest costs; the bisimulation harness (phase 2) is built regardless of the winner.

Independent native UI review found that Pi 0.85.1 showExtensionCustom closes overlay-mode dialogs with unqualified ui.hideOverlay(). Closing a background decision can pop an unrelated newer overlay, leave the closed decision visible, and strand the newer promise. Verified in installed and npm Pi source. The correct narrow fix retains the OverlayHandle returned for this dialog and closes that handle. Add stacked-overlay/background-cancellation and close-before-factory-completion regressions. A Pi host source fix was proposed to John, with no installation/activation. Authorization is pending. Do not work around the ownership bug in ext-pi. Review evidence is in /tmp/agent-cat-pi-native.aK2KKu/review-ext-pi.diff and workflow 1480b945-315b-4b67-b5ab-2e2969068e62.

Shared support for native Emacs goal mttrcv0m-9vzfmn and native Pi goal mttr7s7a-9dc4rh. Session 9ea419cf-1788468379 owns shared runtime/CLI changes in .worktrees/tui and the Emacs client. Session fbe09284-1788937064 owns ext-pi in fork-agent-cat and has acknowledged this boundary.

First slice is implemented: frontend-io reads one version-1 JSON request through stdin EOF, returns one JSON response/newline, and never creates roots, launches runs, or grants live controls. Operations are open-root, read-question, and read-result. Root identities are opaque captured directory identities. Artifact requests use existing references and public identities/codes from validated event streams or restored snapshots, without recomputing expected digests. Verification delegates to Runtime.PrivateRoot and the existing descriptor-relative Store readers. No journal replay is needed for an artifact read. Requests are bounded at 2 MiB and responses at maxArtifactBytes plus 4096. Data/verification failures exit 3 with stderr diagnostics and no JSON response.

Verified source and CLI boundary: /tmp/agent-cat-frontend-io-gate.yGAZMW. Policy and example gates pass in /tmp/agent-cat-frontend-policy.0nXJG9 before an invocation-environment failure at routing-config. The routing gate then exposed one stale assertion for the earlier ACP diagnostic rendering. Its assertion was updated to the actual human-rendered failure prefix while preserving status/no-prompt checks. Routing, ACP and deck gates pass in /tmp/agent-cat-frontend-final-compat.5UGgsS before documentation-registration failures. Final prose/structure/Haskell documentation checks pass in /tmp/agent-cat-frontend-final-docs.mRlcp4. Complete TUI regression gate passes in /tmp/agent-cat-frontend-tui-regression.3hJIxq. Earlier failing logs remain distinct from passing evidence.

Immutable downstream package: /nix/store/55l70rx0ayjvillvqhb9d93kx7b8xagz-agent-workflows. Executable: /nix/store/x1xwkpb42c5bycsdpj083y22lsdyvb11-agent-workflows-0.1.0.0/bin/wf. Shared source snapshot: /nix/store/8xa0jch7s597zps4l87fz3j2y1g1ayxy-source. Build log: /tmp/agent-cat-frontend-wf-package.voRSbJ. Fresh process probes against the source agentic-run and packaged wf/Spanish HelloWorld pass in /tmp/agent-cat-frontend-package-smoke.81h1PX.

Remaining shared scope: descriptor-relative managed input/manifest/state writes and a separate target-aware prepare/start supervision contract. Preserve arbitrary supported target argv, including current/owned/remote Pi ACP proxies and inherited Deck sessions. CLI must own routing fingerprints and bind exact inputs, cwd, target and resolved policy at launch. Env-based target selectors such as remote socket/session must be covered without exposing or persisting secret values. Keep this distinct from read-only artifact access. No TypeScript/Lisp fingerprint reimplementation or generic private-file mutation service.

** Notes

#+begin_example
Prepared-process checkpoint: `frontend` is implemented in cli/src/Agentic/Cli/Frontend.hs, with the registry/target/program callback in Agentic.Cli. It consumes a bounded version-1 preparation frame, retains captured transport bytes and the exact resolved program/policy in the same worker, and accepts only a matching start/discard decision. No input files, lease, run directory, or store are created before start. The private root identity is retained across review. Existing v2 runtime envelopes/controls begin on the same pipe after start. Explicit default scratch arguments are returned/persisted, and a real deterministic ACP regression verifies compatibility with the existing lineage checker. This entrypoint currently creates root runs only. Native lineage and history remain incomplete.

Shared process ownership was moved without implementation changes from Tui.ProcessGroup and tui/cbits/process_group.c to Runtime.ProcessGroup and runtime/cbits/process_group.c, through the public runtime facade. Bounded NDJSON framing and buffered control activation were factored from the existing runtime reader so approval read-ahead cannot lose controls. The public read-only frontend-io operations remain unchanged.

A GHCRTS=-N8 regression exposed a real bootstrap race: Haskell-time fd-3 dup could overwrite an RTS event-manager descriptor and fail with `kevent: invalid argument (Bad file descriptor)`. The existing setup now runs in cli/cbits/control_bootstrap.c before RTS initialization and keeps the same private bootstrap environment contract. No external bridge or executable was added. The eight-capability gate remains enabled. Fresh Linux execution of this constructor change has not yet been established.

Canonical byte/value/digest vectors now live in test/fixtures/runtime/input-sources/v1.json. The native probe exercises 36 prompt/command-tail/stdin and literal/file cases, including empty input, LF/CRLF, Unicode whitespace, BOM, source changes after capture, literal round trips, manifest hashes, and restoration. Other cases cover bad UTF-8, symlink/nonregular/oversized files, malformed/over-bound requests, stale approval/root replacement, opaque ACP and Deck targets without contacting them, buffered cancellation, typed FIFO local human answers, verified results, inherited bootstrap flags, and legacy lineage. A digest-prefix mismatch was caught and fixed before native interface handoff: frontend hashes remain bare hex rather than routing `sha256:` fingerprints.

Passing evidence: /tmp/agent-cat-native-snapshot-lineage.87ledL; /tmp/agent-cat-native-session-compatible.TDvQL9 (policy/examples/routing/ACP/deck/docs); /tmp/agent-cat-native-tui-regression.5dzxCN (full model/render/PTY/lifecycle and default nofile=1048575 descriptor closure); /tmp/agent-cat-native-final-contracts.gmstFu (runtime/native/docs, with a subsequently corrected test-pattern warning); /tmp/agent-cat-native-parser-check.KCAHbV (warning-free build and policy probe, now including both frontend command collisions).

The largest remaining generalization failure: four hand-built KleeneAlgebra instances are all csum-of-powers in a complete idempotent semiring (Cost and Prob literally re-prove the identical formula). One construction over [CompleteCSemiring S] + CsumIsSup deletes both star defs, four star-law proofs, ofCommStarLe, and the reach lemma tower — and yields KleeneAlgebra (Mat S i i) for EVERY idempotent complete carrier, making retry_mat_least construct its algebra instead of assuming it (review finding 12). Wrinkle: leastness needs csum f <= y from pointwise — not derivable from the six fields; CsumIsSup (csum = iSup over CompleteLattice) is the clean mixin, rfl at all three lattice carriers. SUBSUMES acat-9ml and acat-ew8.

Owner, 2026-08-12, after four rounds of example feedback: 'I feel like you haven't understood the nature of this code or how it's meant to be used at all. Re-review Conal's work.' Diagnosis in the doctrine's own terms: every definition the example forced on a workflow author (op inductive, verdict monoid + proofs, carrier abbrevs Sc/Lbl/Wf, decode plumbing, explicit grade annotations) is complexity RELOCATED INTO THE CLIENT — 'a trivially simple abstraction surrounded by machinery has relocated its complexity into its clients, which is worse than having kept it.' Each example round was a failed-morphism diagnostic about the LIBRARY, and was answered by moving text between files instead of repairing the model. Consequences to implement (after owner validates the direction): (1) the author-facing type is the SEMANTIC QUOTIENT (Workflow / WEqR), not raw Term — meanings fix equality, instances live where the laws are true; this puts WEqR congruence (acat-3tu) on the critical path, since Monoid/Category instances require it. (2) Recognized structure, not bespoke vocabulary (anti-pattern 8): reviewers form a Monoid pointwise from Verdict (mu(r1*r2) = convolution, a TCM theorem), so panel := List.prod and 'r1 * r2 * r3' is a legal spelling — the owner asked for exactly this in round 2 ('a list OR a monoidal composition'); >>> is category composition with mu(f>>>g) = mug o muf; gate c is scalar action by Bool (semimodule row of the Step-6 table); revising/loop is a section of kstar; fan is the functorial strength. Each surface combinator's docstring IS its mu-equation, proved next to it. (3) The library owns the one default signature (Prompt = typed text), carriers, Verdict — an author writes ONLY 'ask ...' leaves and their composition; the example file is the English sentence made typeable, ~15 lines. (4) Surface lives in Agentic/ proper (subsumes acat-nov), exporting ONE import for authors. Blocked on owner confirming this reading.

Candidate ground-up story to be tested, not assumed: W = graded free hierarchy (Applicative / Selective? / Monad) on ONE typed consultation effect; meanings = unique homomorphisms out of free structures (TCM 'already paid for'); named constructors (retry/fan/gate/scope/share) become derived forms or natural transformations; sharing possibly definitional via content-addressed worlds (no labels). * TODO [#C] Adapter narration corrupts answers; usage data discarded; denial cost unmeasured
:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       acat-owa
:LABELS:   []
:ISSUE_TYPE: task
:CREATED:  [2026-08-14 Fri 12:55]
:MODIFIED: [2026-08-20 Thu 15:01]
:CREATED_BY: johnw
:SOURCE_REPO: agent-cat
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/src/agent-cat
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

From the live re-test at 3b2ab57. (1) claude-agent-acp prepends model-fallback narration ('**Model fallback:** claude-fable-5 declined this request (cyber); retried with claude-opus-4-8') to the answer stream as ordinary agent_message_chunk text. In one run it was concatenated to a reviewer's 'APPROVE', and because approval must be the WHOLE reply (the fail-closed rule), decodeVerdict read the result as an objection and bought a revision round. Consider: strip a recognised leading narration block before decoding (fragile, and it is the adapter's text not the model's), or record it as a turn annotation, or leave decoding strict and surface the narration in the transcript so a reader knows why a verdict flipped. Note the substituted model (claude-opus-4-8) is NOT in the adapter's advertised catalogue, so Conn.configValues is not a complete account of what may answer. (2) Exec.say discards the usage object claude puts on the wire; capturing tokens per turn would let the runtime report real cost beside the tick bill, and would settle (3). (3) permissionByCode's docstring cites ~97k tokens as the measured cost of denial, but the live refusing run showed 0 extra turns and 0 retries — the figure came from an earlier probe under different conditions. Either measure it properly with usage data or soften the claim to what is known.

scale n (scale 0 f) = bounded n while scale (n*0) f = bounded 0: the composite over-approximates (sound as upper bound; a fan of a 0-fan runs nothing). Any future fanT-fusion/normalization rule holds as scale (n*m) f <= scale n (scale m f), not equality. Record before a normalization pass assumes it.

* DONE [#C] DSL: a diagnosis inside a library carets the program's text — Pos has no module field
:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       acat-pos-module-attribution-oxq
:LABELS:   []
:ISSUE_TYPE: bug
:CREATED:  [2026-08-15 Sat 15:05]
:MODIFIED: [2026-08-18 Tue 13:05]
:CREATED_BY: johnw
:FINISHED: [2026-08-18 Tue 13:05]
:CLOSE_REASON: done
:SOURCE_REPO: agent-cat
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/src/agent-cat
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

Round 16 gave the front end modules (import), so a CheckError can now point into a library's source, but Dsl.Pos carries only line/col. The walk wraps such errors with 'in module `m`:' (walkImports.inMod), which names the file, but the CLI's caretLines still slices the *program's* text at the library's line, so a caret can underline an unrelated line when the position happens to be in range.

The design of record (doc/research/dsl-redesign/fn-import-design.md, 'The one tax that is not local') prescribes the fix: add module : String := "" to Dsl.Pos, stamped by the parser per module, printed by ToString CheckError, added to Mcp.checkErrorJson. Defaulted so structure-instance literals are unchanged, but the ~25 anonymous constructors in Parse.lean need a Pos.at helper, and flagshipRaw/battery position pins recompute.

Until then: the 'in module' prefix is the attribution, and the CLI's caret is best-effort. Acceptance: a syntax error inside an imported library renders with its own file's line quoted (or no caret), never the program's.

Found by the haskell-question analysis (§6.3 item 8, acat-haskell-surface-ygy): RawAsk.model is Option String and askShape applies atModel unconditionally when it is some, so a hand-built Raw with model := some m on a tool or person addressee elaborates instead of being refused — the surface invariant ('served by names the model that serves a model addressee') is parser-only. One site: askShape or a checkBlock-side guard, plus a hand-built battery case in the exception-2 style (check_panel_nil precedent). Notable because it is the ONLY thing the hypothetical Haskell type discipline would catch that the Lean checker does not — closing it makes the redundancy argument unconditional.

* DONE [#B] Bound secure subprocess startup under very large RLIMIT_NOFILE
:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       acat-tbn
:LABELS:   []
:ISSUE_TYPE: bug
:CREATED:  [2026-09-04 Fri 21:17]
:MODIFIED: [2026-09-05 Sat 15:18]
:CREATED_BY: johnw
:FINISHED: [2026-09-05 Sat 15:18]
:CLOSE_REASON: Fixed in bundled process/GHC with native package-set alignment; default-limit Linux build/tests/TUI, Darwin/Linux packages, ownership regressions, scoped reviews and independent completion audit approved.
:SOURCE_REPO: agent-cat
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/src/agent-cat
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

The x86_64-linux Nix shell on andoria-08 supplies soft and hard RLIMIT_NOFILE=1073741816. agentic-run --tui stalls before its first draw while System.Process.createProcess(close_fds=True) walks the descriptor range; the child is still in the pre-exec image and the parent blocks on the exec-error pipe. process-1.6.26.1 documents the O(max-open-files) close loop. Direct descriptor listing returns in 0.008 seconds, whereas TUI startup times out at 45 seconds; lowering limits to 4096 in the verification subshell permits startup. Preserve close_fds=True: do not bypass descriptor hygiene or silently impose a process-wide limit merely for green tests. Resolve bounded spawn and cancellation/reap during spawn through the supported process implementation, with a high-limit regression and Darwin/Linux packaging evidence. The parent cancellation during the diagnostic left its pre-exec helper orphaned; that exact diagnostic child was subsequently terminated.
